chs=60 → runs 20..59 = 40 more epochs. That's correct for "train until epoch 60 total". However, there's a subtlety: eval-every triggers at `(epoch+1) % 5 == 0`. On resume starting at 20, the schedule shifts. That's acceptable — the checkpoints keep coming every 5. Also `best_epoch` semantics: best.pt stores the epoch that achieved best val. If I load best.pt (best at epoch 14) and start at 20, best_val is restored so the patience/early-stop compares correctly against the historical best. Good. But a cleaner resume would continue from the LAST trained epoch (19), not the best (14). The best.pt only has the best state though — we don't have the epoch-19 state saved separately (only best.pt and the final overwrite isn't saved unless it improved). The training code only saves best.pt on improvemen

=== 6191232f-faa1-4571-b88b-90559a5ef758.jsonl:5056 ===
continuing from the BEST model (epoch 14 weights) rather than the last (epoch 19) — we lose epochs 15-19 of training trajectory but keep the best weights. That's actually fine for continuing toward higher MRR (we continue from the best known model). But start_epoch should then reflect that we're continuing from a model that may be older. For early-stopping bookkeeping, resuming from best.pt with best_val/best_epoch restored and starting fresh at some epoch is the standard "restart from best checkpoint" pattern. Let me set start_epoch = best_epoch + 1 conceptually but we can't know how far the last-run went. The cleanest: resume starts a fresh patience counter from best.pt's epoch. 对,这**就是一次(修正后的)重训**——而且必须重训,原因是: **为什么不能沿用旧权重** 之前所有 gamma 结果(Stage4/Stage5 的 11%/10%)是在**有 bug 的训练机制**下训出来的:RPBE 梯度压倒 task(~10⁴×)、LR 调度问题、没有 minibatch replay。这些权重反映的是"坏机制"下的 Gamma,拿它做 RPBE 判决无效。 **Stage5-R 改的是训练机制本身**(Gamma 更新时钟、RPBE 统计窗口、λ、优化器),这些改动只作用于"训练过程",**已训出来的权重里不含这些机制**——所以只能**用新机制重新训**,权重才会反映修正后的优化。 **起点**:两臂都从**同一个 avg best** 起(不是从旧 gamma 权重),这样 avg/gamma-task/gamma-rpbe 同起点、可比。 **"不用重启"是什么意思**:评审说的是"**λ 已经从一开始就固定为 1e-5,不用为了校准中途停掉重启**"——而**不是**"不用重训"。这条 run 本身就是那次重训,λ 一次设好、跑到底即可。 **~6.5h** = 15k 优化步 ÷ ~30 步/分(两臂并行,各占一卡)。
